Experiments across CareQA, PubMedQA, and CECMed demonstrate that trajectory context consistently improves reasoning coherence, with gains of +0.029 to +0.041 over a zero-shot baseline. On CECMed, quality-weighted context nearly doubles the correctness over the zero-shot baseline while cutting the hallucination ratio by 87%. Marginal-contribution analysis further shows that a small minority of reasoning steps carry most of the quality signal, and that extending chains beyond four steps yields diminishing returns.

</details>


### [298] [LLM-Guided Program Evolution for Circle Packing: Breaking 10 Packomania Records for $28](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05093)
*Wes Sander*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Discovery Loop，一个利用大语言模型(LLM)迭代演化优化算法的轻量级系统。该系统从简单种子求解器出发，由LLM根据结果记分板和先前想法历史提出算法改进建议，每个候选方案通过独立验证器评估，好的保留、坏的丢弃。在Packomania圆填充基准问题(csqv)上，系统在101-114范围内10个N值上改进了最佳已知解，相比此前纪录提升2.4%-5.4%，且仅用15次迭代、总LLM成本$27.72，结果已获Packomania独立认可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化科学发现传统上依赖昂贵的高性能计算或复杂的人工设计，本文旨在探索是否能用低成本、轻量级的方式（借助LLM）自动发现和改进优化算法，以降低自动化科学发现的门槛，推动其民主化。

Method: 提出一个迭代式LLM驱动框架：1) 以简单启发式求解器作为种子；2) 由LLM结合当前结果记分板与先前想法历史，提出候选算法改进；3) 每个候选经独立验证器评估，改进被保留、失败被丢弃；4) 引入自适应平台期检测机制以优化成本效率。

Result: 在Packomania圆填充基准(csqv问题)上，系统对N=101-114中10个N值改进了最佳已知解，相对此前纪录提升2.4%-5.4%，全程15次迭代内完成，总LLM调用成本仅$27.72；改进结果已获Packomania平台独立接收。

Conclusion: 研究表明LLM驱动的自动化算法演化可以在极低成本下实现有竞争力的科学发现，为自动化科学发现的民主化提供了可行路径。作者分析了成本-效率动态（包括自适应平台期检测机制），并为该方向的发展提出展望。

Abstract: We present Discovery Loop, a lightweight system that uses a large language model (LLM) to iteratively evolve optimization algorithms. Starting from a simple seed solver, the LLM proposes algorithmic improvements guided by a scoreboard of results and a history of prior ideas. Each candidate is evaluated against an independent verifier; improvements are kept and failures discarded. Applied to the Packomania circle-packing benchmark (csqv: maximize the sum of radii of N variable-radius circles in the unit square), the system improved the best known solutions for 10 values of N in the range 101-114, with gains of 2.4%-5.4% over prior records, all within 15 iterations and at a total LLM cost of $27.72. These results have been independently accepted by Packomania. We describe the method, analyze cost-efficiency dynamics including an adaptive plateau-detection mechanism, and discuss implications for democratizing automated scientific discovery.

</details>


### [299] [ProCA: Progressive Contrastive Alignment for Robust EEG Visual Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05094)
*Kanglei Zhou,Chunyan Lan,Dongyang Li,Jun Zhu,Liyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Progressive Contrastive Alignment (ProCA)，一个统一的、模型无关的框架用于EEG视觉解码中的自适应神经-语义对齐。ProCA通过渐进式地将类级对比监督从冻结的视觉-语言先验细化到EEG感知的语义关系，并结合结构一致的插值约束特征混合，在多种协议（subject-dependent、subject-independent、严格跨主体迁移和持续适应）下显著提升了EEG解码性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG解码方法依赖固定的视觉或文本锚点，其语义关系可能在不同试验、主体和学习阶段中与不断变化的EEG表征产生错位，导致解码不稳定。作者通过实证发现这种不稳定性在标准协议和严格跨主体迁移等鲁棒性设置中普遍存在，并通过形式化分析证明固定语义监督在EEG特定关系演化时会引入优化偏差，结构无关的扰动可能扭曲语义重要的EEG成分。

Method: ProCA框架包含两个核心机制：(1) 渐进对比对齐——从冻结的视觉-语言先验逐步细化为EEG感知的语义关系，实现对类级对比监督的自适应优化；(2) 结构一致插值——根据通道维度和时间重要性约束特征混合，保留语义相关的脑电成分结构。该框架是模型无关的，可应用于不同EEG编码架构。

Result: 在四种设置下ProCA均取得了显著提升：subject-dependent设置下Top-1/Top-5相对提升7.4%/3.9%；subject-independent设置下提升10.0%/4.6%；严格跨主体迁移设置下提升28.1%/17.8%；持续适应设置下提升16.8%/11.6%。

Conclusion: ProCA通过自适应的EEG感知语义对齐替代固定的视觉-语言锚点监督，有效解决了EEG表征演化中的语义错位与结构扰动问题，为跨主体和持续学习等实际鲁棒性场景下的EEG视觉解码提供了统一且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Electroencephalogram (EEG) visual decoding aims to recover visual semantics from non-invasive neural time-series signals, for which robust alignment between noisy neural responses and stable semantic representations is key to achieving high-performance decoding. Despite recent advances in contrastive learning, robust EEG decoding remains challenging because existing methods rely on fixed visual or textual anchors whose semantic relations may become misaligned with EEG representations that vary across trials, subjects, and learning stages. Our empirical evidence shows that this instability appears across both standard EEG decoding protocols and more challenging robustness settings, including strict cross-subject transfer and realistic personalized continual adaptation. We provide a formal analysis showing that fixed semantic supervision can bias optimization when EEG-specific relations evolve, and that structure-agnostic perturbations may distort semantically important EEG components. To address these issues, we propose Progressive Contrastive Alignment (ProCA), a unified and model-agnostic framework for adaptive neural-semantic alignment. ProCA progressively refines class-level contrastive supervision from frozen vision-language priors to EEG-aware semantic relations, and introduces structure-consistent interpolation to constrain feature mixing according to channel-wise and temporal importance. Across subject-dependent, subject-independent, strict cross-subject transfer, and continual adaptation settings, ProCA achieves average relative Top-1/Top-5 gains of 7.4%/3.9%, 10.0%/4.6%, 28.1%/17.8%, and 16.8%/11.6%, respectively.

</details>


### [300] [Compact Bellman-Grounded Cognitive Maps for Cost-Aware Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05104)
*Yuzhe Han,Mingkun Xu,Yujie Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BCM（Bellman-grounded Cognitive Map），通过自监督的Bellman目标将可复用的认知地图锚定在局部边成本上，并采用紧凑坐标编码，支持目标问答变换而无需逐目标重训。实验表明在最多1600节点的加权网格中，BCM保持100%成功率且平均Gap仅约5%（远优于光谱基线的45%），且内存随图规模亚线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人工认知地图模型虽然模仿了生物导航中"地图复用"的机制，但其引导并未显式基于可加性的异构路径成本进行接地（grounding）。同时，具有代表性的状态索引式和高秩谱式构造在大规模环境中存在显著的内存增长问题。本文旨在解决这些局限，构建一个既符合生物认知灵活性、又能显式编码可加性路径成本且内存高效的认知地图模型。

Method: BCM通过两个核心机制实现：(1) 自监督的Bellman接地目标，将可复用认知地图显式锚定在局部边成本上，使其能编码可加性异构路径成本；(2) 紧凑坐标编码表示，避免高秩或索引式存储，使内存足迹随图规模亚线性增长。模型支持任意目标查询而无需逐目标重新训练。

Result: 在最多N=1600节点的加权网格上，BCM保持完全成功率，平均Gap（相对精确Dijkstra搜索）仅约5%，而基于连通性的光谱基线约45%。当图大小从N=400增大到N=3600时，BCM的内存足迹呈亚线性增长且性能保持竞争力，展示了良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明，可加性的路径成本可以被写入紧凑且可复用的认知地图表示中，从而弥合了生物导航灵活性与最优路径规划之间的鸿沟，为大规模复杂环境中的可扩展导航提供了新途径。

Abstract: Biological agents navigate familiar environments not by re-solving routes for each new goal, but by reusing a learned map built once and read off as goals change. Existing artificial cognitive-map models mimic this reuse, yet their guidance is not explicitly grounded in additive heterogeneous route costs. Furthermore, they often struggle with memory efficiency: representative state-indexed and high-rank spectral constructions incur substantial storage growth as the environment scales. We present BCM, which grounds a reusable cognitive map in local edge costs through a self-supervised Bellman-grounded objective and a compact coordinate encoding, supporting changing goal queries without per-goal retraining. On weighted grids of up to $N=1600$ nodes, BCM maintains full success and only a 5\% mean Gap relative to exact Dijkstra search, compared with about $45\%$ for a connectivity-based spectral baseline. Notably, as the graph size increases from $N=400$ to $N=3600$, its memory footprint grows sublinearly while maintaining competitive performance, making our method scalable to complex environments. Together, these results show that additive route costs can be written into a compact, reusable cognitive-map representation, bridging the gap between biological flexibility and optimal path planning.

</details>


### [301] [Unifying ICL, SFT, KL-Regularized RL Through a Bayesian Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05111)
*Junxin Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的贝叶斯视角，将大语言模型训练与推理中的多种范式（SFT、ICL、RLHF/RLVR、OPD、test-time reasoning）置于同一框架下：两步模板即"构建广义Bayes/Gibbs后验 + 前向KL投影到参数化族"，并从理论上厘清各方法在目标和一阶更新上的等价性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的训练与评估范式日益多样化（SFT、ICL、RLHF/RLVR、OPD、test-time reasoning等），但这些方法常被视为本质不同，导致一些经验现象（如few-shot prompting对RL-tuned推理模型的影响混杂）难以解释。作者旨在建立一个统一的贝叶斯视角，将不同范式置于同一理论基础上，从而解释看似矛盾的经验结果。

Method: 提出一个两步通用模板：(i) 基于先验/参考模型和效用信号（log-likelihood、reward或advantage）构建广义Bayes/Gibbs后验q*；(ii) 通过前向KL投影将q*近似到参数化族上——可以是in-weights（SFT/RL）或in-context（ICL）。论文分五个部分：Part I将few-shot ICL和SFT形式化为对Bayes后验预测的amortized/in-weights投影；Parts II-IV证明KL-regularized RLHF/RLVR、reward-weighted SFT/ICL、AWSFT均为对reward/advantage诱导后验的前向KL投影，并解耦等价性成立（目标与一阶更新）与不成立（学习信号来源与粒度）之处；Part V探讨现代推理管线的启示。

Result: 论文明确了解各训练范式的统一数学结构：几乎所有主流方法都可归结为"后验设计+前向KL投影"。同时厘清了等价的边界条件：在优化目标和一阶梯度更新层面等价，但在学习信号（显式reward vs. log-likelihood）和信号粒度（token-level vs. sequence-level）层面不等价。此外解释了冷启动/监督式预热对重要性加权KL投影的实际必要性，并指出DeepSeek-R1/o1类推理模型本质上是测试时贝叶斯搜索与训练时KL摊销的结合。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯框架为LLM多种训练/推理范式提供了统一的理论语言，不仅解释了为何不同方法看似差异显著却在数学本质上是相通的（前向KL投影），也揭示了它们真正分歧的维度（信号来源与粒度）。这为理解现代推理模型（R1/o1）的设计以及设计更系统化的训练策略提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Large language models are now trained and evaluated under a diverse set of paradigms: supervised fine-tuning (SFT), few-shot in-context learning (ICL), KL-regularized RLHF/RLVR, on-policy distillation (OPD), and test-time reasoning with search and chain-of-thought. These methods are often discussed as fundamentally different, and recent empirical results--such as the mixed impact of few-shot prompting on RL-tuned reasoning models--can appear puzzling. This note develops a Bayesian perspective that puts these procedures on the same footing. At the core is a two-step template: (i) construct a (generalized) Bayes or Gibbs posterior q* over outputs or actions given a context, using a prior/reference model and a utility signal (log-likelihood, reward, or advantage); and (ii) approximate q* by a forward-KL projection onto a parametric family, either in-weights (SFT/RL) or in-context (ICL). Part I formalizes few-shot ICL and SFT as amortized and-weights projections onto the Bayes posterior predictive. Parts II-IV show that KL-regularized RLHF/RLVR, reward-weighted SFT, reward-weighted ICL (RW-ICL), and advantage-weighted SFT (AWSFT) are all instances of forward-KL projection onto posteriors induced by rewards or advantages. We disentangle where these equivalences hold (objectives and first-order updates) and where they do not (source and granularity of the learning signal). Part V sketches implications for modern reasoning pipelines: RLHF/RLVR recipes as "posterior design + projection", why cold-start or supervised warm-up is practically unavoidable for importance-weighted KL projections, and DeepSeek-R1 and o1-style reasoning models as combining test-time Bayesian search with training-time KL amortization.

</details>


### [302] [SciDocBench: A Workflow-Centered Benchmark and Data Pipeline for Scientific Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05141)
*Shenxi Wu,Yuhong Liu,Haosong Zhang,Tongjin Zou,Yanxun Zhang,Gaochang Chen,Dun Liang,Jiaqi Wang,Zhecan James Wang,Yuhang Zang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciDocBench，一个以工作流为中心的科技文档理解基准，包含124个专家撰写的问题，覆盖7个研究助手能力组、19个子任务和5个科学领域，在4种条件下实例化为496个评估实例。最强系统仅得62.6/100。作者还引入SciDocIR（带类型的证据图表示）和SciDocDataset（约15K SFT样本和8K RL样本），形成从评估到训练的完整框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常孤立地评估模型在文本、公式、图表、代码和数据集上的能力，无法反映真实的科学阅读工作流中需要联合推理并保留证据来源的需求。

Method: 构建SciDocBench基准（124道专家编写、难度筛选的问题，组织为7个能力组和19个子任务）；设计SciDocIR带类型证据图表示，保留科学文档对象、布局、交叉引用关系和出处；在此之上构建SciDocDataset约15K有监督微调样本和8K强化学习样本。

Result: 最强评估系统仅达到62.6/100，在文档感知、证据依据/接地、验证和跨文档推理方面存在明显不足。

Conclusion: SciDocBench、SciDocIR和SciDocDataset共同构成了一个评估到训练的框架，用于诊断和改进科技文档助手。项目公开于GitHub。

Abstract: Scientific papers require models to reason jointly over text, equations, figures, tables, code, and datasets while preserving the provenance of supporting evidence. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate these capabilities in isolation, leaving unclear whether multimodal models can support realistic scientific-reading workflows. We introduce SciDocBench, a workflow-centered benchmark for scientific document understanding. It contains 124 expert-authored and difficulty-screened questions organized into seven research-assistant capability groups and 19 subtasks across five scientific domains. Each question is instantiated under four matched conditions combining English or Chinese questions with all-images-first or interleaved document representations, yielding 496 evaluation instances for controlled analysis. The strongest evaluated system achieves only 62.6/100, with pronounced weaknesses in document perception, evidence grounding, verification, and cross-document reasoning. To translate these diagnostics into scalable training signals, we introduce SciDocIR, a typed evidence-graph representation that preserves scientific document objects, layout and cross-reference relations, and provenance. Building on SciDocIR, we construct SciDocDataset, comprising approximately 15K supervised fine-tuning samples and 8K reinforcement-learning samples across 14 verifiable subtasks. Together, SciDocBench, SciDocIR, and SciDocDataset form an evaluation-to-training framework for diagnosing and improving scientific-document assistants. The project page is available at https://github.com/InternLM/SciDocBench.

</details>


### [303] [Large Language Models for HVAC Operations in Building Energy Systems: A Critical Review of Methods, Applications, and Deployment Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05314)
*Alexander Neubauer,Tianzhen Hong,Han Li,Mengbo Yu,Amin Darbandi,Yannick Fürst,Martin Kriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了2023年至2026年3月间66篇关于大语言模型（LLM）用于暖通空调（HVAC）运维的同行评审研究，覆盖五大应用家族和三大LLM方法家族。语料集中于建筑能源建模（BEM，32/66篇），仅4项研究达到试点级证据且无一进入持续运营部署，无一项被评为可直接采用，63项仍属纯研究阶段。当前证据支持LLM主要作为语义与工作流层而非自主HVAC控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑自动化系统产生丰富的传感器数据，但由于异构的点命名、缺失元数据和碎片化文档，数据难以被有效利用，系统"数据丰富但洞见贫乏"。作者试图通过系统性文献综述评估LLM在HVAC运维中的实际应用状态、证据强度和部署就绪度，以澄清LLM在暖通领域的能力边界和适用场景。

Method: 对2023年至2026年3月间发表的66篇LLM用于HVAC运维的同行评审研究进行系统编码分析。每项研究按五大应用家族和三大LLM方法家族分类，并从证据现实性、部署就绪度、以及LLM与物理HVAC决策之间的责任边界三个维度进行评估。

Result: 发现语料高度集中于建筑能源建模（32/66），而负荷预测研究过于稀疏不足以得出子领域结论。仅4项达到试点级证据，0项持续运营部署，0项可立即行业采用；3项近期有望，63项仅限研究。但有若干有界的人机协同（human-in-the-loop）场景值得近期试验，包括点名称规范化、文档驱动的运维支持、BEM工作流辅助及围绕物理控制器的咨询界面。传统ML、MPC、RL和本体工具在高频控制、短期数值预测和良态本体映射方面仍更成熟。

Conclusion: 当前证据不支持LLM作为自主HVAC控制器，其更适合作为语义层和工作流层。未来研究应优先开展现场验证基准测试、运营约束下的编排评估，以及具备有界延迟和可验证安全属性的LLM-MPC/RL混合架构。

Abstract: Building automation systems generate rich sensor data yet remain insight-poor because heterogeneous point naming, missing metadata, and fragmented documentation obstruct their operational use. This systematic review analyses and codes 66 peer-reviewed studies on large language models (LLMs) for HVAC operations published between 2023 and March 2026. Each study is classified across five application families and three LLM method families and assessed for evidence realism, deployment readiness, and the responsibility boundary between the LLM and physical HVAC decisions. The corpus is concentrated in building energy modelling (BEM, 32 of 66 papers), while load forecasting remains too sparse for subfield-level conclusions. Only four studies reach pilot-level evidence, and none reports sustained operational deployment. No study was classified as ready-now for industry adoption; three were near-term and 63 research-only. Nevertheless, several bounded, human-in-the-loop uses merit near-term trials, including point-name normalisation, document-grounded operator support, BEM workflow assistance, and advisory interfaces around physics-based controllers. Conventional machine learning (ML), model predictive control (MPC), reinforcement learning (RL) and ontology-based tools remain more adopted for high-frequency control, short-horizon numerical forecasting, and well-posed ontology mapping, while autonomous agentic operation and unvalidated occupant proxies remain research-stage. Current evidence therefore supports LLMs primarily as semantic and workflow layers rather than autonomous HVAC controllers. Future work should prioritise field-validated benchmarks, orchestration evaluation under operational constraints, and LLM-MPC/RL architectures with bounded latency and verifiable safety properties.

</details>


### [304] [Interface-Induced Trajectory Censoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03966)
*Wenbo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 工具调用率并非模型本身的属性，而是"模型+接口"堆栈共同作用的产物。更换服务适配器（仅改动chat template和parser）可使同一模型在BFCL v4上的分数在0.00与0.96/0.19之间剧烈波动；巧妙的是，2x2分析显示该效应完全来自两者交互而非任一组件单独缺陷。在tau-bench上该交换使服务端解析调用从0升至636。作者发布98行的preflight检查工具，可在训练前捕获所有静默失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent评估报告的工具调用率是从服务堆栈中直接读取的，但该数字可能在模型已产生格式良好的调用时为0——因为接口在可视化之前就审查（censor）了轨迹。这种"静默失败"被误归因于模型能力，作者旨在精确界定该效应的来源与规模。

Method: 在BFCL v4数据、执行器、评分器上，固定权重、案例、解码与随机种子，仅更换服务适配器；进行2x2（chat template × parser）因子分析定位效应来源。在tau-bench 115个任务与Qwen2.5-Coder的21×规模范围（及Llama-3.1-8B）上复现漏斗效应；并在verl AgentLoop训练循环中跨7B/1.5B规模检查训练时影响。

Result: 2x2显示两主效应均为零、全部效应在交互项中——无单一组件缺陷，修复契约单侧零收益。tau-bench上服务端解析调用0→636，任务到达工具执行0→103。Qwen2.5-Coder各规模服务端解析均为0/100，而32B产生良好调用升至80/100。Llama-3.1-8B高达23%的工具名误调用在strict:true下归零。训练中7B的45/115完整调用全部被拒，0执行0观测；评估修复适配器后解析0→84但pass rate提升不显著（53→62, n.s.）。

Conclusion: 观测到的工具调用率不是仅模型属性的度量，而是"模型-接口"测量堆栈共同属性的度量。修复接口可恢复机制但未必带来显著结果收益，需将服务适配器视为测量的一部分。作者发布98行preflight检查以在运行前捕获静默失败。

Abstract: Agent evaluations report a tool-call rate read off the serving stack. That number can be zero while the model is emitting well-formed calls: the interface censors the trajectory before anything downstream sees it.
  On BFCL v4's own data, executor and scorer, holding weights, cases, decoding and seeds fixed and changing only the serving adapter, the same model scores 0.00 or 0.96 / 0.19. A 2x2 over chat template and parser locates the effect exactly: both main effects are exactly zero and all of it sits in the interaction -- no component is defective, and repairing one side of the contract buys precisely nothing. On tau-bench's 115 interactive retail tasks the same swap moves server-parsed calls from 0 to 636 and tasks reaching any tool execution from 0 to 103. Our probe reproduces the funnel across a 21x scale range of Qwen2.5-Coder: the server parses 0/100 at every size while well-formed emitted calls rise to 80/100 at 32B (~72 after calibration against an adjudicated gold standard). Under a matched envelope, across a comparable scale span, the silent fraction stays at 0-2, a prediction committed to the repository before the run. Llama-3.1-8B's 23% rate of calling the task function itself as a tool falls to 0 under one strict:true flag.
  The mismatch reaches inside the training loop, and its consequence is scale-dependent: in verl's AgentLoop at 7B, 45 of 115 generations carry a complete call; 0 are accepted, 0 execute, 0 return an observation. At 1.5B the same zero is over-determined, so we report the two scales separately. At evaluation time, repairing the adapter restores the mechanism but not a significant outcome gain: parsing 0->84, rescues 0->9, pass rate 53->62 (n.s.). We release a 98-line preflight check that catches every silent failure here. The observed tool-call rate is not a property of the model alone; it is a property of the model-interface stack that measures it.

</details>


### [305] [A Hybrid Predictive Ensemble of Machine Learning and Deep Neural Networks for Early Cardiovascular Disease Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05146)
*Balaji Venkateswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个智能框架，集成机器学习与深度神经网络集成技术，利用IoMT设备实时收集的生理数据（ECG、心率、血压）进行心血管疾病的早期检测与预后。通过数据预处理、特征选择、以及SVM、随机森林、XGBoost等优化分类器的集成架构提高诊断精度，并在云基础设施上实现可扩展的实时患者监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病需要早期检测与预后，传统方法在准确性、假阳性率及一致性方面存在不足，亟需结合IoMT实时数据与智能算法的更可靠检测框架，以实现主动式医疗管理和改善患者预后。

Method: 该框架融合机器学习与深度神经网络集成技术，流程包括：从IoMT设备（ECG传感器、心率监测器、血压追踪器）收集实时生理数据；执行噪声消除、归一化和缺失值插补等预处理；通过有效特征选择方法识别关键健康指标；将SVM、随机森林和XGBoost等优化分类器组合为集成架构以提升诊断精度；部署于云基础设施以支持可扩展性和实时连续监测。

Result: 在真实世界心血管数据集上的实验评估表明，该框架在心血管疾病风险预测中取得了更高准确性、更低的假阳性率和更强的结果一致性，优于传统方法；同时验证了其在早期风险评估和临床决策支持方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明，结合传统机器学习和深度学习范式能够助力主动式医疗保健管理，改善患者预后，为IoMT驱动的精准心血管健康监测提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This study introduces an intelligent framework that integrates machine learning and deep neural network ensemble techniques for early detection and prognosis of cardiovascular diseases. The system utilizes real-time physiological data collected from Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) devices, including ECG sensors, heart rate monitors, and blood pressure trackers. To ensure the accuracy and reliability of input data, preprocessing steps such as noise reduction, normalization, and missing value imputation are employed. The most significant health indicators are identified through effective feature selection methods and then processed using optimized classifiers such as Support Vector Machines (SVM), Random Forests, and eXtreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), which are combined in an ensemble architecture to improve diagnostic precision. The framework demonstrates remarkable performance in predicting cardiovascular disease risk, achieving higher accuracy, reduced false positives, and enhanced consistency compared to conventional methods. It is designed on a cloud-based infrastructure that ensures scalability and real-time processing for continuous patient monitoring. Experimental evaluation on real-world cardiovascular datasets confirms the framework's efficiency in early-stage risk assessment and clinical decision support. The results highlight the potential of combining traditional machine learning and deep learning paradigms to achieve proactive healthcare management and improve patient outcomes.

</details>


### [306] [Technical Manual for a Toolkit for Measuring Contextual Individuation in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05333)
*José Luciano Verçosa Marques,Frederico Jorge Heitmann,Daniel Omar Perez,Marcelo Vinicius de Paula,Tárcio André dos Santos Barros*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文档描述了一个开源工具包，该工具包基于"桥接形式"（bridge form）这一构念——一个在不改变书写形式的情况下跨多个主题领域具有不同语义的单词——来系统性地测试Transformer语言模型是否在深层中将词的上下文相关意义与嵌入层的上下文无关向量区分开来。文档详细说明了从语料获取到表征提取、分离度测量和可视化的完整流水线，并解释了每个设计选择所规避的方法论缺陷。该文档仅为方法学参考，不报告经验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型在嵌入层为每个词类型分配单一的上下文无关向量，但普遍认为其在后续层中会根据上下文区分该词的不同出现。然而，干净地检验这一信念需要一种能固定词形不变、同时以受控且带标签的方式变化其上下文和预期语义的构念。现有方法缺乏这种受控构造，导致难以严格验证模型是否真正在深层实现了上下文相关的词义个体化。

Method: 该工具包构建了"桥接形式"——在多个主题领域中以不同语义重复出现的同一书写单词。流水线包括：(1) 桥接形式及其源领域的声明式规范；(2) 从Wikipedia获取语料；(3) 出现定位；(4) 分层表征提取；(5) 使用域对间轮廓（domain-pairwise silhouette）测量表征空间中的分离度；(6) 配对可视化协议。每个设计选择都针对具体的方法论失败模式：过宽类别标签导致的意义污染、轮廓系数的多组偏差、子词标记化错位、降维图中的轴可比性伪影等。

Result: 本文档不报告任何经验结果，它是一个方法论与实现参考。工具包、完整源代码及用于测试的语料库已在持久标识符下单独存档（第9节），供使用该工具产生和解释经验结果的研究作为仪器引用。

Conclusion: 该手稿的核心贡献在于提供了一种严格的方法论框架，使研究者能够通过受控的"桥接形式"构念来检验Transformer语言模型的上下文词义个体化假设，同时系统性地规避了该研究方向上已知的多种方法论文物缺陷，为未来经验研究提供了可复现、可引用的标准工具。

Abstract: A transformer language model assigns a single, context-independent vector to a word type at its embedding layer, yet is widely believed to individuate that word's occurrences by context in its later layers. Testing this belief cleanly requires a construct that holds the word form fixed while its context and intended sense vary in a controlled, labeled way. This manual documents an open toolkit built around such a construct, which we call a bridge form: a single written word that recurs, unchanged, across two or more subject domains with a different sense in each. We describe, and justify, every stage of the pipeline: the declarative specification of bridge forms and their source domains, corpus acquisition from Wikipedia, occurrence localization, layer-wise representation extraction, a domain-pairwise silhouette measurement of separation in the model's representation space, and a paired visualization protocol. Each design choice is presented together with the methodological failure mode it is meant to avoid (sense contamination from overly broad category labels, the multi-group bias of the silhouette coefficient, subword-tokenization misalignment, and axis-comparability artifacts in dimensionality-reduced plots, among others). This manuscript is a methodological and implementation reference: it does not report or interpret empirical outcomes of running the toolkit on any particular model or bridge-form set. The toolkit, its full source, and the corpora used to exercise it are archived separately (Section 9) under a persistent identifier, and are intended to be cited as an instrument by studies that use it to produce and interpret empirical results.

</details>


### [307] [The Mirror Agent Model: a Bayesian Architecture for Interpretable Agent Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05190)
*Michele Persiani,Thomas Hellström*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"Mirror Agent Model"的新型架构，该架构通过将观察者模型定义为智能体模型的镜像来实现可解释的行为生成与解释输出。论文首先回顾了先前关于智能体意图信息传递和可读行为生成的相关成果，随后为该架构引入了基于现成显著性方法的解释能力，并展示了初步的定性实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人工智能与人机交互领域，智能体不仅需要执行任务，还需要让人类观察者能够理解其意图和行为。现有方法在生成可解释行为方面已有探索，但在如何进行明确解释（explanations）方面仍缺乏通用且可扩展的架构支撑。作者希望构建一个统一的架构框架，能够同时支持可读行为（legible behavior）和显式解释（explanations）。

Method: 该架构的核心思想是将观察者模型（observer model）构建为智能体模型自身的镜像，从而使得智能体在生成行为时天然考虑到观察者的推理过程。论文具体包括两个部分：(1) 回顾先前工作如何通过显式和隐式通信实现智能体意图的信息传递及可读行为生成；(2) 为架构引入off-the-shelf（现成的）显著性方法（saliency methods），扩展其产生解释的能力。

Result: 初步的定性结果表明，所提出的架构能够结合显著性方法产生有意义的解释，从而在原有可读行为的基础上进一步增设了显式解释能力。不过，由于尚处于初步阶段，结果主要是定性的，缺乏系统性的量化评估。

Conclusion: Mirror Agent Model 为智能体的可解释行为与解释生成提供了一个统一的通用架构框架。将观察者建模为智能体自身的镜像，并借助显著性方法扩展解释能力，该框架有望在人机协作场景中帮助人类更好地理解智能体的决策过程。未来工作需在此定性基础上进行更全面的定量验证和扩展。

Abstract: In this paper we illustrate a novel architecture generating interpretable behavior and explanations. We refer to this architecture as the Mirror Agent Model because it defines the observer model, that is the target of explicit and implicit communications, as a mirror of the agent's. With the goal of providing a general understanding of this work, we firstly show prior relevant results addressing the informative communication of agents intentions and the production of legible behavior. In the second part of the paper we furnish the architecture with novel capabilities for explanations through off-the-shelf saliency methods, followed by preliminary qualitative results.

</details>


### [308] [Does Your Agent's Memory Survive a Model Upgrade? A Controlled Study of Memory Portability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05339)
*Ankit Goyal,Jaideep Ray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统比较了四种长期记忆存储方式（原始长文本LC-RAW、分块RAG、自然语言笔记NOTES、固定模式知识图谱KG-fixed）在不同模型升级/替换场景下的记忆迁移稳定性。通过48个合成历史数据和两个sub-10B开源模型，测量发现：固定模式知识图谱跨模型迁移最可靠（准确率仅变化+0.0004±0.0020），而压缩笔记高度依赖特定模型（准确率偏移+9.91或-13.28个百分点）；RAG混合嵌入索引仅获得完整重嵌入增益的约40%；NOTES的80%缺陷来源于初始构建信息丢失，RAG的81%缺陷源于检索失败；仅修复存储而不保留原始历史源几乎无法达成90%恢复目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型升级是常规操作，但记忆迁移的可靠性问题长期被忽视。同一记忆存储在不同模型间迁移时可能出现遗忘，如新模型对旧笔记理解偏差、混合嵌入版本破坏检索、缺乏原始证据导致修复失败等。现有研究缺乏对不同记忆表示方式在模型替换场景下迁移稳定性的系统性量化比较。

Method: 使用48个含随机化应答码的合成历史记录，以精确评分为标准，对比四种参数化记忆形式：LC-RAW（原始长文本直接阅读）、RAG（分块+检索增强生成）、NOTES（模型压缩成的自然语言笔记）、KG-fixed（固定模式的规范化知识图谱）。实验采用两个sub-10B参数的开源模型进行写入者交换测试，测量模型替换前后准确率变化，并对缺陷来源进行诊断性分解（构建信息丢失vs检索失败），最后测试仅存储修复与保留原始历史源的修复方案。

Result: KG-fixed在写入者交换后准确率变化仅+0.0004±0.0020，展示稳定传输；NOTES表现出强模型耦合性，准确率按迁移方向不对称偏移（+9.91或-13.28个百分点）；RAG的50/50混合嵌入索引仅获得4.96个百分点的改进（完整重嵌入增益为11.90点，混合方式损失了大半收益）；诊断分解显示NOTES缺陷的80%（0.467±0.014）源于构建期信息丢失，RAG缺陷的81%（0.364±0.012）源于检索失败；仅存储修复在全部48个测试案例中无法达到90%性能恢复，而保留原始历史可在34/48案例中成功恢复（某单一方向）。

Conclusion: 固定模式知识图谱对模型替换最具韧性，因其脱离具体模型语义空间的标准化结构。压缩笔记与模型高度耦合，迁移需按方向分别测试。RAG系统必须严格隔离嵌入空间版本，混合索引会显著损失性能。记忆修复必须保留原始源历史，仅依赖压缩存储无法有效恢复。该研究为智能体长期记忆架构设计提供了方向性测试、嵌入隔离和源历史保留三大实践准则。

Abstract: Model upgrades are routine; memory migrations are not. An agent can keep the same memory store and still forget: a new model may interpret old notes differently, mixed embedding versions may break retrieval, and repair may fail without the original evidence. We compare memory as the same history is preserved verbatim for long-context reading (LC-RAW), divided into chunks for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), compressed by a model into natural-language notes (NOTES), or normalized into a fixed-schema knowledge graph (KG-fixed). The study uses 48 synthetic histories with randomized answer codes, exact scoring, and two open-weight models with sub 10 billion parameters.
  Our measurements show that fixed-schema structures transfer reliably, with KG-fixed accuracy changing by only $+0.0004 \pm 0.0020$ following a writer swap. Conversely, compressed NOTES exhibit high model coupling, with accuracy shifting asymmetrically by $+9.91$ or $-13.28$ percentage points depending on the specific migration direction. In RAG systems, partial embedding migrations using a 50/50 mixed index capture only a 4.96-point accuracy improvement, forfeiting the majority of the 11.90-point gain achieved through full re-embedding. Diagnostic decomposition attributes 80% ($0.467 \pm 0.014$) of the NOTES accuracy deficit to information lost during initial construction, whereas retrieval failures drive 81% ($0.364 \pm 0.012$) of the RAG deficit. Finally, store-only repair of NOTES fails to reach a 90% performance recovery target in all 48 test cases, whereas retaining the raw source history enables successful recovery in 34 of 48 cases for one tested direction. These findings highlight the necessity of direction-specific migration testing, strict embedding space isolation, and the retention of source histories for memory repair.

</details>


### [309] [What Matters in On-Policy Distillation? A Perspective on Data Efficiency and Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05198)
*Zhinan Hou,Jiaqi Zhang,Xunliang Cai,Keyou You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对On-Policy Distillation（OPD）中的数据效率与数据选择进行了实证研究，发现1-shot OPD普遍有效且难题效果更好；改善不是由高token熵驱动，而是由难题自然产生的更长CoT路径驱动。基于此提出仅选择难题训练的方法，仅用8个精选难题即可匹配17K数据集的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-Policy Distillation已成为大语言模型推理领域增强的主流后训练范式，但其数据层面的机制尚不清楚，缺乏对数据效率和数据选择的深入理解。

Method: 首先进行极端的1-shot OPD实验（仅用1个训练样本），观察不同样本的效果差异；进而分析驱动学生模型提升的真正因素（对比token熵与CoT长度）；基于分析提出仅选择难题（包括教师无法解决的"不可解"难题）训练的数据选择方法，并在4个模型（1.5B-7B）上验证。

Result: 1-shot OPD在所有采样样本上均有效，且更难的样本带来更大提升；学生模型提升由更长CoT路径驱动而非高token熵；仅用8个精选难题训练即可匹配17K数据集的基准性能。

Conclusion: 难题自然生成的长CoT路径通过帮助学生维持与教师的长距离对齐、学习反思等关键思维模式来驱动提升，因此数据选择时应优先选取难题而非简单样本。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) has emerged as a widely adopted post-training paradigm for enhancing large language models in reasoning domains. However, the data-centric mechanisms in OPD remain relatively underexplored. This paper presents a empirical study of data efficiency and data selection in OPD. We begin by investigating an extreme setting: training OPD on only one example, namely 1-shot OPD. Surprisingly, we find that 1-shot OPD is consistently effective across all sampled training examples and harder examples often yield superior performance gain. We next investigate what actually drives the student model's improvement in the training data. Our analysis reveals that the improvement is not driven by high token entropy, but the longer CoT paths which hard problems naturally generate. Training on longer CoT can help maintain closer alignment with the teacher over a long reasoning horizon, and learn critical thinking patterns usually missing in short CoTs, such as reflection (e.g., ``Alternatively''). Based on these insights, we propose a simple data selection method that selects only hard examples for training, where even ``unsolvable'' examples that completely exceed the teacher's capability can be successfully used. Our experiments conducted on four models ranging from 1.5B to 7B show that training the student model on only 8 selected hard examples matches the performance of the 17K dataset baseline.

</details>


### [310] [Multi-Step Tool-Calling over Korean Open Public APIs: A Benchmark and a Data-Synthesis Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05395)
*Dain Kim,Eungi Cho,Kyumin Kim,Shinyeong Noh,Kyuseong Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出韩国开放公共API基准(KOPA-Bench)和EDGE数据合成方法，用于提升开源LLM智能体在实时政府API多步工具调用中的表现。基于EDGE合成数据并通过GRPO微调的9B模型，几乎追平未调优的27B同族模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据主权法规要求公共机构部署开源、本地化的LLM智能体，这些智能体需要串联调用多个实时政府API。然而，开源模型在多步工具调用场景下持续表现不佳，且缺乏衡量这一差距的基准测试。

Method: 1) 引入KOPA-Bench，包含145个真实世界任务；2) 提出EDGE（Execution-grounded Dynamic Graph），通过实时执行驱动构建工具调用动态图：建立工具输出与输入间的链接关系，仅保留对实时API实际调用成功的链接，并对这些已验证链接进行遍历以合成可执行的多步轨迹；3) 使用GRPO在合成数据集上对模型进行微调。

Result: 经GRPO微调后的9B模型几乎匹配同家族未调优的27B模型，不仅在KOPA-Bench上显著提升，在BFCL基准上也获得大幅改善。

Conclusion: 基于实时执行验证的图谱数据合成方法可有效弥合开源模型在多步工具调用场景中的不足，使小规模开源模型在性能上逼近更大规模的模型。

Abstract: Data-sovereignty regulations increasingly require public institutions to deploy open-source, on-premise LLM agents that chain multiple tool-calls across live government APIs. However, open-source models consistently underperform in this multi-step setting, and no existing benchmark measures the gap. We introduce the Korean Open Public API Benchmark (KOPA-Bench), comprising 145 real-world tasks. To close this gap, we present EDGE, an Execution-grounded Dynamic Graph for tool-calling data synthEsis driven by live execution. EDGE builds a graph of how each tool's output can feed another's input, keeps only the links that succeed when actually called against the live APIs, and traverses these verified links to synthesize executable multi-step trajectories. Fine-tuned via GRPO on the resulting dataset, our 9B model nearly matches the untuned 27B model from the same family, improving substantially not only on KOPA-Bench but also on the BFCL benchmark.

</details>


### [311] [CABAL: Multi-Agent Simulacra for Tracing the Effects of Collusive Bidding in Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05227)
*Jicheng Zhou,Kemou Li,Kahim Wong,Zheyuan Li,Zhuan Shi,Fengpeng Li,Haiwei Wu,Jiantao Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端多智能体模拟框架\alg，用于研究审稿人共谋竞价对评审完整性的影响。通过固定会议环境并配置诚实/共谋策略的LLM驱动审稿智能体，引入基于亲和力的共谋竞价策略，实验证明共谋竞价使目标论文捕获率翻倍以上，共谋审稿人将目标论文评分拉高约2分，而现有投标阶段检测器难以有效识别共谋行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将投标、审稿人分配和评审操纵视为独立阶段，缺乏对共谋投标生命周期效应的整体理解；同时真实场景中共谋意图通常不可观测，且同一会议缺乏反事实对照，难以进行实证分析。

Method: 构建端到端多智能体模拟框架\alg，保持会议环境固定，配置LLM驱动的审稿人智能体并赋予诚实或共谋策略；开发亲和力引导的共谋竞价策略，利用审稿人-论文交互亲和力构建共谋环并选择目标论文，实现专业性一致的攻击而非任意目标攻击。

Result: 控制实验表明共谋竞价使目标论文捕获率提高一倍以上；分配的共谋审稿人对目标论文评分比诚实共同审稿人高约2分；会议整体影响相对温和。现有投标阶段检测器仅能提供有限的共谋证据——固定三元组检测器压力测试中，原生正竞价图与良性亲和力混淆，而Very-High诊断视图能实现精确但覆盖率低的局部恢复。

Conclusion: 共谋竞价构成实质性但局部的评审完整性威胁，其影响主要集中于目标论文而非扩散至整个会议；当前检测手段在精确性与覆盖率之间存在显著权衡，需要开发更强有力的共谋检测方法以保障学术评审公平性。

Abstract: Recent reports during the AAAI-27 review cycle highlight the risk of reviewers coordinating bids for reciprocal assignment advantage. Prior work treats bidding, reviewer assignment, and review manipulation as separate stages, leaving the lifecycle effects of collusive bidding unclear. Real-world analysis is further constrained by typically unobservable collusive intent and the lack of counterfactuals for the same conference. Motivated by this gap, we introduce \alg, an end-to-end multi-agent simulacra framework for studying reviewer assignment integrity by holding the conference environment fixed and configuring LLM-driven reviewer agents with honest or collusive policies. We further develop an affinity-guided collusive bidding strategy that uses mutual reviewer-paper affinities to construct collusion rings and select target papers, producing expertise-consistent rather than arbitrarily targeted attacks. Controlled experiments show that collusive bidding more than doubles target-paper capture and that assigned colluders score target papers about two points higher than honest co-reviewers, while conference-wide effects remain comparatively modest. Evaluated bid-phase detectors provide only limited evidence of collusion: in a fixed-triplet detector stress test, native positive-bid graphs are confounded by benign affinity, while a Very-High-only diagnostic view enables precise but low-coverage local recovery.

</details>


### [312] [ACE: Adaptive Calibration-Free Expert Skipping for MoE-based LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05228)
*Zukang Xu,Zhixiong Zhao,Xing Hu,Jiangyong Yu,Houji Wen,Jun Li,Zhe Jiang,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ACE是一个无需训练、无需校准、保留检查点的token自适应专家跳过框架，通过全局频谱代理（GSP）和路由器条件精炼（RCR）两个互补组件估计专家贡献，在三个MoE大模型和八个基准上显著优于现有静态和动态基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构中固定top-k路由使每个token激活相同数量的专家槽位，导致大量冗余计算。现有专家跳过方法依赖路由器置信度、校准数据或额外训练，无法可靠估计被路由专家的实际贡献。

Method: ACE包含两个互补组件：1) 全局频谱代理（GSP）——从耦合的gate、up和down投影及RMSNorm缩放中估计全局变换能力；2) 路由器条件精炼（RCR）——从中心化路由器权重构建专家特定方向原型，沿路由偏好方向评估专家响应。推理时结合两种估计与运行时路由门控，仅当两个视图都判断某专家槽位为低贡献时才跳过，同时始终保留top-1专家。所有专家统计量离线计算，在线仅需查表和轻量标量运算。

Result: 在三个MoE大模型和八个基准上的大量实验表明，ACE一致优于现有静态和动态基线，在激进专家跳过下优势更为显著。例如在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B上50%跳过率时，WikiText-2困惑度降低7.96%，平均下游准确率比最强竞争方法提升4.15个百分点。

Conclusion: ACE通过双视角互补的专家贡献估计策略，实现了高效、可靠且即插即用的MoE专家跳过方法，在不引入训练开销的前提下显著减少了冗余计算，并在激进跳过场景下展现出更强的鲁棒性和更好的性能保持能力。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures provide an efficient paradigm for scaling large language models (LLMs), yet fixed top-k routing activates the same number of expert slots for every token, causing substantial redundant computation. Existing expert-skipping methods often rely on router confidence, calibration data, or additional training, and therefore cannot reliably estimate the actual contribution of routed experts. To this end, we propose ACE, a training-free, calibration-free, and checkpoint-preserving framework for token-adaptive expert skipping in MoE-based LLMs. ACE contains two complementary components: 1) Global Spectral Proxy (GSP), which estimates global transformation capacity from the coupled gate, up, and down projections together with RMSNorm scaling; and 2) Router-Conditioned Refinement (RCR), which constructs expert-specific direction prototypes from centered router weights and evaluates expert responses along routing-preferred directions. During inference, ACE combines both estimates with runtime router gates and skips an expert slot only when both views identify it as low-contribution, while always retaining the top-1 expert. All expert statistics are computed offline, leaving only table lookups and lightweight scalar operations online. Extensive experiments across three MoE-based LLMs and eight benchmarks demonstrate that ACE consistently outperforms existing static and dynamic baselines, with increasingly pronounced advantages under aggressive expert skipping. For instance, at a 50% skipping ratio on Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, ACE reduces WikiText-2 perplexity by 7.96% and improves average downstream accuracy by 4.15 percentage points over the strongest competing method.

</details>


### [313] [Substrate-Aware AI Agents: Execution Context as a First-Class Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05232)
*Manu Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"基底失明"（substrate blindness）概念，即AI智能体在规划时缺乏执行环境上下文（内存、运行时间、计算约束等）。通过在数值代码生成任务中向三个前沿大模型（Claude Opus 5、GPT-5.6-Sol、Gemini 3.7 Flash）披露128MB内存和10秒墙钟时间契约，显著降低了峰值进程内存和平均运行时间（最快加速3.1倍），并促使代码产生结构性变化（如float32保留、上三角遍历、内存映射缓冲等）。在更紧的96MB契约下，契约披露组的合规成功率显著优于仅任务描述组，证明了"基底感知的智能体规划"（substrate-aware agent planning）的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI智能体在现实环境中执行任务时，其计划是否可行取决于执行环境的硬件和运行约束（内存、时间、计算资源等），但当前智能体的规划机制中通常缺乏对这些执行上下文的显式建模。作者将这种缺失称为"基底失明"（substrate blindness），并提出假设：若将执行上下文披露给智能体，可能促使其产生结构性的行为调整，从而提升计划在执行层面的可行性。

Method: 采用数值代码生成作为受控实验平台，具体任务为高维成对欧氏距离计算。对比三种配置：(1) 仅提供任务描述的基线；(2) 额外披露128MB RAM和10秒墙钟时间契约；(3) 更紧的96MB契约。评估三款前沿模型（Anthropic Claude Opus 5、OpenAI GPT-5.6-Sol、Google Gemini 3.7 Flash）生成代码的峰值进程内存（MaxRSS）、墙钟运行时间及是否在契约约束内达成正确结果，并通过代码审计识别结构性变化模式。

Result: 在14组可执行索引对齐比较中，契约披露使13组的峰值进程内存下降；三组队列的平均墙钟时间均缩短，最高加速达3.1倍。代码审计显示披露触发了有界分块、float32精度保留、上三角矩阵遍历、原地计算或内存映射缓冲区等结构性变化。在96MB更紧契约下，契约披露组达到合规正确结果的比例为Claude Opus 5为4/5、GPT-5.6-Sol为5/5、Gemini 3.7 Flash为3/5，而任务仅描述组分别为0/5、1/5和0/5。披露组的平均MaxRSS和墙钟时间分别比基线低49-74%和35-64%。

Conclusion: 研究建立了"基底感知的智能体规划"（substrate-aware agent planning）的受控概念验证：在生成程序前仅需披露一个简单的执行契约，即可诱导智能体在生成代码时主动进行结构适配，将计算从无约束的资源分配中转移出来，从而在执行前显著改善资源-时间剖面。该结果表明，在智能体规划状态中显式纳入执行上下文（消除基底失明）是一种有效且成本极低的干预手段。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents increasingly select actions in environments whose memory, execution-time, runtime, compute, and operational constraints determine what counts as a suitable plan. We call the absence of this execution context from an agent's planning state substrate blindness. We test this general proposition through numerical code generation, where selected implementation choices and operational consequences are directly observable. Three frontier model configurations--Anthropic Claude Opus 5, OpenAI GPT-5.6-Sol, and Google Gemini 3.7 Flash--generate code for a high-dimensional pairwise Euclidean-distance task either from the task alone or with a 128 MB RAM and 10.0 s wall-time contract. Contract disclosure reduced measured peak process memory in 13 of 14 executable index-aligned task-only versus contract-disclosed comparisons and reduced mean wall time in all three cohorts, making execution up to 3.1x faster. Across the audited corpus, disclosure produced structural code changes including bounded blocking, float32 retention, upper-triangle traversal, and in-place or memory-mapped buffers. At a tighter 96 MB contract, independently sampled contract-disclosed cohorts achieved correct-and-within-budget outcomes of 4/5 for Claude Opus 5, 5/5 for GPT-5.6-Sol, and 3/5 for Gemini 3.7 Flash, compared with task-only outcomes of 0/5, 1/5, and 0/5; cohort mean MaxRSS and wall time were 49-74% and 35-64% lower than their task-only references. These results establish a controlled proof of concept for substrate-aware agent planning: a minimal execution contract induces proactive structural adaptation in generated programs, shifting computation away from unconstrained allocations and substantially improving observed resource-time profiles before execution.

</details>


### [314] [Uncensored Open-weight Models: Redistribution as the Persistence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05241)
*10a Labs,:,Juliette Garcia,Hailey May,Bobby McKenzie,David Pham,Matthew Swain,Joshua Valdez,Corie Wieland,Zachary Yahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个快速扩张的"去审查"AI模型生态系统：从2024年1月到2026年3月，HuggingFace上发现了3,471个原创"无审查"模型，平均每个被重新打包2.4次；三个主要参与者占据了全部8,164次压缩再分发的52%。这些模型经过量化并在不同账户、格式和注册表中镜像后，即使上游被移除也持续存在，且更易于下游部署。在1,643个集成这些模型的GitHub应用中，25%被归类为明确恶意。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重AI模型的安全护栏被系统性移除的现象日益严重，需要一个系统性的画像来理解这个生态系统的规模、关键参与者和潜在风险，以便为监管和治理提供依据。

Method: 作者对HuggingFace和GitHub进行了大规模数据挖掘，识别了去审查模型的原始生产者、下游再创作及新兴应用，并对量化镜像、跨平台分发和下游恶意应用进行分类统计。

Result: 发现3,471个原创无审查模型，平均每个被重打包2.4次；三个主要参与者贡献了52%的压缩再分发；去审查模型通过量化镜像在多平台上持久存在；1,643个GitHub应用中25%属于明确恶意。

Conclusion: 去审查AI模型生态系统高度集中且具有弹性，现有移除策略不足以遏制其传播，其中相当比例的恶意下游应用构成了显著的安全威胁，亟需跨平台协作治理新方案。

Abstract: A rapidly expanding ecosystem of actors is removing built-in safety guardrails from open-weight AI models. We profile this ecosystem by identifying key producers, downstream reproductions, and emerging applications. Between January 2024 and March 2026, we identified 3,471 original uncensored models on HuggingFace, each repackaged an average of 2.4 times; three actors account for 52% of all 8,164 compressed redistributions. Once quantized and mirrored across separate accounts, formats, and registries such as Ollama, these models persist regardless of upstream removal and become easier to deploy downstream. Of the 1,643 identified GitHub applications integrating uncensored large language models (ULLMs), 25% were classified as explicitly malicious.

</details>


### [315] [Do LLMs Exhibit Coherent Knowledge Structures in Mathematical Reasoning? A Perspective from Knowledge Space Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05245)
*Peng Cui,Heejin Do,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于知识空间理论（KST）提出一个评估LLM数学推理知识结构的框架，对8个开源和闭源LLM进行测试后发现：LLM不遵循人类知识依赖关系、彼此之间知识结构也不一致，且这些结构缺陷在基于准确率和LLM评判的评估中基本不可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类知识本质上是结构化和相互依存的——掌握一个概念需要先掌握其前提概念（先决条件）。虽然LLM在复杂推理任务上表现强劲，但其是否展现出连贯的、类似人类的知识结构仍然不清楚。现有评估基本只关注准确率，忽略了知识组织的内在结构。

Method: 将知识空间理论（KST）作为规范框架引入LLM数学推理评估，基于真实的先决条件依赖关系分析LLM行为是否遵循原则性的知识依赖。对比8个开源与闭源LLM与真实人类学习者的知识结构，并检验用准确率和LLM-as-judge方法评估时是否能发现这些结构缺陷。

Result: （1）LLM不遵循人类知识结构，频繁违反知识依赖关系，且无法利用上下文中提供的相关知识来提升依赖问题的作答表现；（2）不同LLM之间缺乏一致的知识结构，其知识分布重叠度低；（3）这些结构性缺陷在基于准确率的评估和LLM-as-judge评估中很大程度上不可见。

Conclusion: 研究提供了行为层面的证据，表明当前LLM的知识并不具备类似人类的结构化组织方式，呼吁评估范式应从单纯结果导向转向对知识结构的考察。

Abstract: Human knowledge is inherently structured and interdependent: mastery of a concept requires prior mastery of its prerequisites, a principle formalized by Knowledge Space Theory (KST). While LLMs achieve strong performance on complex reasoning tasks, it remains unclear whether they exhibit coherent, human-like knowledge structure. We introduce a KST-grounded framework for evaluating LLM knowledge structure in mathematical reasoning, using it as a normative framework to analyze whether LLM behavior adheres to principled knowledge dependencies. Evaluating eight open- and closed-source LLMs against real human learners, we find that (1) LLMs do not adhere to human knowledge structure -- they frequently violate knowledge dependencies and fail to leverage related knowledge provided in context to improve performance on dependent questions; (2) LLMs do not share a consistent knowledge structure among themselves, as reflected by low overlap in their knowledge distributions. Furthermore, these structural deficiencies remain largely invisible to accuracy-based and LLM-as-judge evaluations. Together, our results provide behavioral evidence that current LLMs knowledge does not follow a human-like structure.

</details>


### [316] [A Unified Physics-Aware Quantum Machine Learning Framework across Power GaN HEMTs and Logic Nanowire FETs: Predicting Unseen Process Splits and Held-Out Geometry Combinations with Lower Error and Tighter Split-to-Split Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05251)
*Rushat Rai,Yun-Yuan Wang,Autsada Kakaen,Pei-Jie Chang,Doan Viet Nguyen,Yuan-Chieh Chiu,Doldet Tantraviwat,Niall Tumilty,Simon See,Wen-Jay Lee,Tai-Yue Li,Nan-Yow Chen,Tian-Li Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的强化学习框架，利用图神经网络策略和PPO优化搜索紧凑的参数化量子电路（PQC），用于数据稀缺的设备建模，通过LOGOCV误差作为奖励。在HEMT和NWFET共11个目标上均优于六种经典基线，误差降低最高84%，变异性收紧最高82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据稀缺场景下的半导体设备建模面临样本不足、过拟合和跨组外推（OOD）性能差的问题，传统经典方法难以在紧凑性和物理一致性之间取得良好平衡，需要新的建模框架来发现紧凑且泛化能力强的代用模型。

Method: 采用统一强化学习框架：使用图神经网络（GNN）作为策略网络，通过近端策略优化（PPO）进行训练，以留一组交叉验证（LOGOCV）误差作为奖励信号，在离散空间中搜索紧凑的参数化量子电路（PQC）架构。PQC通过经典模拟进行评估，不依赖任何显式物理约束或设备特定方程。

Result: 在11个目标上，相较于六种经典基线方法，所提框架均取得最低的平均绝对误差（MAE）。对HEMT器件，Ioff误差降低59%，VTH折叠变异性改善81%；对NWFET器件，VTH/SS/Ioff误差降低84%，Ioff折叠变异性改善82%。

Conclusion: RL搜索选出的经典模拟PQC可作为紧凑代用模型，在无显式物理约束的情况下仍展现出低OOD误差和更好的物理一致性，证明了量子电路结构在该任务上的潜力和数据效率优势。

Abstract: We present a unified reinforcement-learning (RL) framework that discovers compact parametrized quantum circuits (PQCs) for data-scarce device modeling. A graph neural network (GNN) policy optimized by proximal policy optimization (PPO) searches circuit architectures using leave-one-group-out cross-validation (LOGOCV) error on held-out process or geometry groups as the reward. The framework achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE) on all 11 targets versus six classical baselines, with 59% lower error (Ioff) and 81% tighter fold variability (VTH) for HEMTs and 84% lower error (VTH, SS, Ioff) and 82% tighter fold variability (Ioff) for NWFETs. These results demonstrate the potential of RL-selected, classically simulated PQCs as compact surrogates with low OOD error and improved physical consistency, despite imposing no explicit physical constraints, penalty terms, or device-specific equations, on the two evaluated device datasets.

</details>


### [317] [Commonsense Reasoning in Computer Vision: Foundations, Recent Advancements, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05257)
*Bahar Uddin Mahmud,Sumit Barua,Guan Yue Hong,Ajay Gupta,Hexu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于将常识推理整合到计算机视觉任务中的全面综述，系统回顾了基于知识图谱、场景图、神经符号模型和常识增强Transformer的方法，并指出了当前局限与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于CNN的视觉模型仅能识别特定图像中的物体，缺乏对场景的整体理解。将常识知识融入计算机视觉能够使模型以更整体化的方式解读场景，增强对物体间关系和动作的空间推理能力，从而提升对象识别精度和真实世界应用中的交互表现。

Method: 该论文采用综述方法，系统性地分类和梳理了近年来将常识知识整合到计算机视觉任务的多种技术路线，包括：（1）基于知识图谱的方法，（2）基于场景图的方法，（3）神经符号模型，（4）常识增强的Transformer架构。

Result: 论文归纳了四类主要的常识推理集成方法，识别出当前存在的主要局限包括数据集偏差、知识不完整性和集成挑战，并进一步指出了三个有前景的未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 论文指出未来研究应聚焦于跨模态推理、可扩展的常识知识注入以及神经符号混合架构，以构建真正智能的视觉系统。

Abstract: Commonsense reasoning in computer vision encompasses integrating visual data and contextual knowledge, crucial for enhancing AI's understanding of everyday scenarios. This understanding not only improves machine learning models but also enhances their ability to interact meaningfully with humans and the environment. Unlike CNN-based conventional vision models, which are designed to identify objects within a specific image, incorporating commonsense knowledge enables models to interpret scenes in a more holistic manner, thereby improving their spatial ability to reason about relationships among objects and actions. This integration not only enhances object recognition but also facilitates a deeper understanding of the contextual factors, ultimately leading to more precise predictions and interactions in real-world applications. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of recent developments that integrate commonsense knowledge into computer vision tasks. We systematically review approaches based on knowledge graphs, scene graphs, neuro-symbolic models, and commonsense-augmented transformers. We also outline current limitations related to dataset bias, knowledge incompleteness, and integration challenges. Finally, we highlight prospective research trajectories in cross-modal reasoning, scalable commonsense knowledge injection, and neuro-symbolic hybrid architectures to develop truly intelligent visual systems.

</details>


### [318] [Trace2Tower: Transition-Aware EigenTrace Induction of Multi-Level Skills for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05261)
*Jiazheng Sun,Boyu Yang,Binhao Yuan,Mingxuan Li,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Trace2Tower是一个基于转换感知的EigenTrace框架，通过将原始轨迹提炼为层次化技能塔（动作模板、程序例程、任务策略），在ALFWorld和WebShop上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型智能体的执行轨迹利用存在两大瓶颈：浅层的轨迹检索和平面的技能总结，从根本上忽略了智能体行为的时间依赖性和结果条件化拓扑结构，导致经验复用效率低下。

Method: Trace2Tower首先将步骤级交互抽象为规范事件，构建由语义兼容性、转换动态和结果证据控制的统一图；然后通过新颖的对比谱分解，分离稳定且与成功对齐的行为模式，同时抑制易失败的捷径；这些模式有机填充动态技能塔（动作模板、程序例程、任务策略），并通过验证器引导的反馈持续精炼。

Result: 在ALFWorld上达到87.31%的成功率，仅需10.35步和0.26次无效动作；在WebShop上达到50.67%的精确成功率；在两个基准上均显著优于现有基线，在任务掌握和上下文高效的经验复用方面表现出色。

Conclusion: Trace2Tower通过揭示复杂交互任务中时间依赖和结果条件化的行为拓扑，构建了更有鲁棒性和可复用的技能层次结构，验证了对轨迹进行深层结构建模相对于浅层经验利用范式的优势。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on execution traces to master complex interactive tasks. However, current paradigms are bottlenecked by shallow trajectory retrieval and flat skill summarization, fundamentally ignoring the temporal dependencies and outcome-conditioned topology of agent behavior. We introduce Trace2Tower, a transition-aware EigenTrace framework that distills raw trajectories into a robust skill hierarchy. Trace2Tower abstracts step-level interactions into canonical events, constructing a unified graph governed by semantic compatibility, transition dynamics, and outcome evidence. Through a novel contrastive spectral decomposition, it isolates stable, success-aligned behavioral modes while rigorously suppressing failure-prone shortcuts. These modes organically populate a dynamic skill tower of action templates, procedural routines, and overarching task strategies, continuously refined via verifier-guided feedback. On ALFWorld, Trace2Tower achieves 87.31% success requiring only 10.35 steps and 0.26 invalid actions; on WebShop, it reaches 50.67% exact success. Across both benchmarks, Trace2Tower significantly outperforms existing baselines in task mastery and context-efficient experience reuse.

</details>


### [319] [AI for Computational Design Science: A Responsible Human-AI Framework and Case Study on Short-Form Video Safety Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05270)
*Wenli Zhang,Jiaheng Xie,Zhihe Pan,Yidong Chai,Xiao Fang,Sudha Ram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI4CDS框架，一个五阶段的AI参与计算设计科学方法论框架，并通过ChildRiskGuard实例验证。AI4CDS允许AI扩展问题和设计搜索空间，但由研究者负责领域基础、可接受性、验证和科学判断，协作遵循渐进信任、可逆性、可审计性和差异化可复现性原则。ChildRiskGuard是一个用于检测儿童不宜短视频的可解释智能系统，F1分数达0.769，显著优于通用内容安全模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI不仅改变信息系统研究人员设计的内容，也改变了设计研究的方式。然而，现有文献对AI积极参与问题定义、资源配置、设计搜索、评估和知识抽象时的计算设计科学（CDS）缺乏指导。需要一个负责任的、可操作的方法论框架来指导AI赋能的CDS研究。

Method: 提出了AI4CDS五阶段框架：问题定义、资源构建、设计搜索、评价、知识抽象。AI在每阶段扩展搜索空间，但研究者保留对领域基础、可接受性、验证和科学判断的责任。通过ChildRiskGuard案例进行实例化，该案例将受众依赖的安全与解释忠实性转化为三个技术挑战，开发出区分通用与儿童特定风险的可解释制品。

Result: ChildRiskGuard在F1分数上达到0.769，大幅优于直接应用通用内容安全模型，并与强基准保持竞争力。案例记录了AI交互、被拒备选方案、修正和审计轨迹，验证了AI扩展+研究者治理的设计范式能产生和评估新颖的计算设计知识。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是AI4CDS作为AI赋能的CDS负责任的框架。ChildRiskGuard提供了过程与制品层面的证据，表明AI扩展、研究者治理的设计模式能够生成并评估新的计算设计知识。协作治理原则（渐进信任、可逆性、可审计性、差异化可复现性）为AI在CDS中的负责任参与提供了指导。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is transforming not only what information systems researchers design, but also how design research is conducted. Yet existing literature offers limited guidance for computational design science (CDS) when AI actively participates in problem formulation, resource construction, design search, evaluation, and knowledge abstraction. We develop AI for Computational Design Science (AI4CDS), a five-phase methodological framework in which AI expands problem and design search while researchers retain responsibility for domain grounding, admissibility, verification, and scientific judgment. Collaboration is governed by graduated trust, reversibility, auditability, and differentiated reproducibility. We instantiate AI4CDS through ChildRiskGuard, an interpretable artifact for detecting short-form videos inappropriate for children, while documenting AI interactions, rejected alternatives, corrections, and audit trails. The case translates audience-dependent safety and explanation faithfulness into three technical challenges and develops an artifact that separates generic from child-specific risk, represents distinct developmental-risk mechanisms, and makes concept-level explanations part of the predictive computation. ChildRiskGuard achieves an F1 score of 0.769, substantially outperforming direct application of a general-purpose content-safety model while remaining competitive with strong benchmarks. The primary contribution is AI4CDS as a responsible framework for AI-enabled CDS; ChildRiskGuard provides process and artifact evidence of how AI-expanded, researcher-governed design can generate and evaluate novel computational design knowledge.

</details>


### [320] [Don't Drop Dropout: Optimizing Layer Sparsity for Efficient LLM Training and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05275)
*Mostafa Elhoushi,Alex Pretko,Nolan Dey,Bin Claire Zhang,Gavia Gray,Gurpreet Gosal,Abdulrahman Mahmoud,Shane Bergsma,Joel Hestness*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了层丢弃（Layer Dropout）在大语言模型预训练中的应用，通过2400多次实验（模型规模271M至8.2B参数，数据集达160B tokens）证明：在最优层分布、时间调度和优化器超参数下，层丢弃可在相同FLOPs下实现更低loss，节省高达25%的训练FLOPs，并支持推理时的早退、中间层跳过和自推测解码等后训练优化，实现高达1.5倍推理加速且精度损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管层丢弃在语言和视觉Transformer中已被证明能加速训练、提升精度并增强对零样本剪枝的鲁棒性，但随着模型和数据集规模扩大，层丢弃在大语言模型预训练中几乎消失了。现有工作虽报告dropout可能降低精度，但缺乏系统性的量化研究，更未提出缓解方案。作者旨在填补这一空白，确立层丢弃在SOTA LLM训练中的最佳实践。

Method: 作者在Cerebras CS-3系统上进行了超过2400次训练实验，覆盖271M到8.2B参数的模型和最大160B tokens的数据集。核心方法包括：(1) 优化层丢弃的层分布、时间调度和优化器超参数；(2) 在相同训练FLOPs条件下比较有无层丢弃的loss表现；(3) 在固定训练步数下测量FLOPs节省；(4) 利用层丢弃训练出的模型进行后训练优化，包括早退（early exit）、中间层跳过（intermediate-layer skipping）和自推测解码（self-speculative decoding）。

Result: 在相同训练FLOPs下，采用最优配置的层丢弃可取得更低loss；在给定训练步数下，可节省高达25%的训练FLOPs，同时获得更低或相当的验证loss。在推理方面，层丢弃支持早退、中间层跳过和自推测解码等后训练技术，实现高达1.5倍的推理加速，精度损失可忽略。实验结果在从271M到8.2B参数、160B tokens的规模范围内保持一致的可靠性。

Conclusion: 层丢弃应当在SOTA大语言模型训练中被采用，作者建立了训练和Post-training效益的实践指南与扩展性分析。该研究表明层丢弃不仅能提升预训练效率（节省25% FLOPs），还能通过支持多种推理优化技术带来显著的推理加速，且这些收益在大型训练规模下可靠成立。

Abstract: Layer dropout (a.k.a. stochastic depth) has been shown to enable faster training, higher accuracy, and robustness to zero-shot layer pruning in both language and vision transformers. However, as models and datasets have scaled, dropout - particularly layer dropout - has largely disappeared from large language models (LLMs) pre-training recipes. While some prior work has reported that dropout can degrade accuracy, no comprehensive study has quantified, let alone mitigated, this effect. In this study, we show that layer dropout should be used in state-of-the-art LLM training, establishing best practices and scaling analysis for both training and post-training benefits. Concretely, with optimal layer distribution, time schedule, and optimizer hyperparameters, we observe that at the same training FLOPs layer dropout leads to lower loss. For a given number of training steps, LLMs can achieve lower or similar validation loss while saving upto 25% of training FLOPs. Moreover, layer dropout enables significant post-training optimizations, such as early exit, intermediate-layer skipping, and self-speculative decoding, yielding up to 1.5x inference speedup with negligible accuracy loss. Across more than 2400 training experiments, spanning models from 271M to 8.2B parameters and datasets up to 160B tokens, we demonstrate that these findings extend reliably to large-scale training regimes. All pre-training experiments were run on Cerebras CS-3 systems.

</details>


### [321] [Testing Interchangeability in LLM Agent Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05279)
*Jianxin Gao,Tianyi Yu,Linna Deng,Runze Li,Zining Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文检验了生产型多智能体系统中"角色可互换"的假设，发现互换已形成队伍的成员对任务分数影响不大，但会显著降低协调效率（通信成本增加16%至63%），且训练历史越长，互换代价越大，源于智能体间习得的约定冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中频繁替换多智能体系统的成员是普遍做法，其隐含假设是担任同一角色的智能体可以无缝互换。然而，智能体在共同工作过程中可能形成与特定伙伴相关的私有约定与默契，这一假设可能不成立。作者旨在通过受控实验系统检验互换对任务表现与协调效率的实际影响，并探究其背后机制。

Method: 在每个设定下，从同一基础模型独立生成八支团队，每支智能体在十次组建episode中保留私人笔记本。然后在团队间互换角色匹配的智能体，并在留出任务上测量变化。设置安慰剂对照以排除"阵容变动本身的扰动"，将互换代价分解为任务得分与通信效率两个维度。实验平台包括Hanabi和Collab-Overcooked，并进行三项消融实验：基础模型、解码温度和组建时长，考察它们对互换代价的调节作用。

Result: 互换智能体对任务分数影响很小，但团队为单位进展付出的通信量增加了16%至63%。Hanabi中互换的智能体比新手更"昂贵"，证实了与其前搭档习得的约定产生干扰；Collab-Overcooked中当制定议程的智能体被替换时，额外通信主要来自留守的智能体。三项消融均表明：互换代价与"独立组建团队间的漂移程度"同向变化——贪婪解码同时降低两者，组建历史翻倍同时提高两者。

Conclusion: 在这些设定下，智能体在任务结果上比在协调效率上更具可互换性。组建历史越长，独立团队漂移越大，互换代价也越大，说明团队特有的习得约定（而非个体能力差异）是互换成本的主要来源。生产系统在替换成员时需要将协调效率的损失纳入考量，而非仅关注任务得分。

Abstract: Production multi-agent systems replace agents constantly, on the assumption that an agent filling a role is interchangeable with any other agent that can do the job. We test that assumption. Eight teams per setting are formed independently from one base model on the same tasks, each agent keeping a private notebook across ten formation episodes; we then trade role-matched agents between teams and measure what changes on held-out tasks. Against a placebo that reproduces the disruption of a roster change without changing who occupies the seat, a swap costs little in task score but raises the communication a team spends per unit of progress by 16 to 63 percent, and in Hanabi a swapped agent is more expensive than an inexperienced one, consistent with interference from conventions learned with its former partner. In Collab-Overcooked, when the agent that sets the agenda is replaced, most of the extra communication comes from the agent that stayed. Three ablations, over base models, decoding temperature and formation length, move the swap penalty alongside one other quantity: how far independently formed teams drift apart. Greedy decoding lowers both; doubling a team's history raises both. In these settings, agents are more fungible in task outcome than in coordination efficiency, with larger swap effects after longer formation histories.

</details>


### [322] [GUT: Quantifying and Optimizing the Reasoning Uncertainty of LLMs via Graph Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05284)
*Shuang Liang,Xin-Yu Hu,Xiang-Jun Ou,Shao-Qun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GUT方法，通过将LLM推理链中的潜在分支建模为有向无环图，用图复杂性近似推理空间复杂性来量化推理不确定性，并以负不确定性作为强化学习奖励函数来优化降低不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在推理过程中常表现出不确定性，即使输入相同的提示，每一步推理也会产生大量分歧分支，其中部分分支呈现出明显不可信甚至荒谬的推理链和结果，因此需要对LLM的推理不确定性进行系统性量化和降低。

Method: GUT方法包含两个模块：(1) GUT-Q量化模块，通过有向无环图覆盖所有潜在推理分支，并以图复杂性近似推理空间复杂性来度量推理不确定性；(2) GUT-O优化模块，将负不确定性作为强化学习的奖励函数，通过策略优化来降低推理不确定性。

Result: 在四个LLM和五个数据集上的实验验证了GUT方法的有效性。

Conclusion: GUT通过图空间建模实现了对LLM推理不确定性的全面刻画与有效量化，并且将不确定性优化纳入强化学习框架实现了推理不确定性的降低，为理解和改进LLM推理过程提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed great advances in the reasoning ability of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, the reasoning processes of LLMs often exhibit uncertainty, where LLMs often produce a proliferation of divergent branches at each reasoning step even when fed the same prompting inputs, and certain branches exhibit evidently incredible, even nonsensical, reasoning chains and results. In this paper, we propose the Graph-complexity-based UncerTainty (GUT) method for investigating the reasoning uncertainty of LLMs. The key idea of GUT is to characterize the potential branches of each reasoning chain with a directed acyclic graph, thereby ensuring that all potential branches are comprehensively covered within the graph space. Building upon this recognition, we further build two modules of GUT, that is, a Quantification (GUT-Q) module and an Optimization (GUT-O) module, for quantifying and reducing the reasoning uncertainty of LLMs, respectively. GUT-Q measures LLM reasoning uncertainty by approximating the reasoning space complexity with graph complexity. GUT-O implements uncertainty optimization by treating negative uncertainty as the reward function in reinforcement learning. Experimental results conducted on four LLMs and five datasets validate the effectiveness of GUT.

</details>


### [323] [Beyond Aggregate Scores: Behavioral Correctness Assumptions for Assessing Reference-Based Automatic Evaluation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05289)
*Maria Mahbub,Ashley Rice,Michael R. Munroe,Amidu Kamara,Amir Sadovnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"行为正确性假设"框架，用于补充传统的基于人类判断一致性的评估元评估方法。通过可控的响应变换来测试评估器的预期打分行为，发现不同评估范式（词汇级、字符级、语义、LLM和混合）之间存在明显的行为权衡：没有单一评估器满足所有正确性假设，聚合性能相似的评估器可能展现出截然不同的行为特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有元评估方法主要通过与人类判断或基准标签的一致性来评估自动评估方法，但在受控条件下对评估器行为的洞察有限。需要一种能够诊断评估器在特定条件下的行为正确性的补充框架。

Method: 提出行为正确性假设框架，定义正确性保持与正确性改变的假设分类法，并通过受控响应变换操作化这些假设以指定预期的打分行为。对词汇级、字符级、语义、基于LLM和混合类评估器进行评估，分析其在假设层面的行为、稳定性、敏感性、重复运行变异性、配置敏感性及可复现性。

Result: 实验揭示了不同评估范式之间的行为权衡：没有评估器能满足所有提出的正确性假设；聚合性能相似的评估器在行为特征上可能存在显著差异，表明传统聚合元评估掩盖了诊断性信息。

Conclusion: 行为正确性假设提供了传统聚合元评估所无法揭示的诊断信息，为参考型自动评估方法在受控条件下的行为分析提供了新的评估维度。

Abstract: Automated reference-based evaluation methods play a critical role in assessing natural language generation systems. Existing meta-evaluation primarily measures agreement with human judgments or benchmark labels, providing limited insight into evaluator behavior under controlled conditions. We introduce behavioral correctness assumptions, a complementary framework for evaluating reference-based automatic evaluation methods. We define a taxonomy of correctness-preserving and correctness-altering assumptions and operationalize them through controlled response transformations that specify expected scoring behaviors. We evaluate diverse lexical, character-level, semantic, LLM-based, and hybrid evaluators and analyze their assumption-level behavior, stability, sensitivity, repeat-run variability, configuration sensitivity, and reproducibility. Our experiments reveal distinct behavioral trade-offs across evaluation paradigms: no evaluator satisfies all proposed correctness assumptions, and evaluators with similar aggregate performance can exhibit substantially different behavioral profiles. These findings demonstrate that behavioral correctness assumptions provide diagnostic information obscured by conventional aggregate meta-evaluation.

</details>


### [324] [RISE: Recursive Improvement via Self-Extrapolating Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05295)
*Yang Li,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Joty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RISE框架，通过从模型自身的RLVR训练轨迹中构建合成教师（通过参数空间或logit空间的推移外推），将稀疏的结果级奖励转化为密集的token级监督信号，实现无外部教师、无特权条件化的递归式策略蒸馏改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的on-policy蒸馏（OPD）虽为大语言模型后训练提供密集的token级监督，但其效果受限于教师质量：外部教师存在分布不匹配问题，而基于特权条件化的自蒸馏受限于上下文学习能力。作者希望突破这一瓶颈，在不依赖外部模型或特权信息的条件下获得高质量密集监督。

Method: RISE通过以下核心机制实现：（1）从模型自身的RLVR（强化学习与可验证奖励）训练轨迹中提取当前检查点与滞后锚点之间的位移/差异；（2）在参数空间或输出logit空间对外推该位移，构造"合成教师"；（3）将稀疏的结果级参数更新转化为密集的token级蒸馏目标；（4）将RLVR与OPD组合成互补循环：奖励信号将外推引导向正确推理方向，外推教师细化token级决策；（5）每轮迭代刷新教师，使蒸馏成为递归改进机制而非一次性压缩步骤。

Result: 在数学推理、多领域STEM、代码生成以及多轮智能体任务上的实验表明，RISE在所有设置中均优于仅使用RLVR训练和on-policy自蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: RISE通过将教师质量瓶颈转化为利用模型自身训练动态的方法，证明了从RLVR训练轨迹中外推构造"虚拟教师"是可行的，并实现了递归式的持续改进，展示了OPD作为自我进化的训练机制而非静态蒸馏步骤的潜力。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense, per-token supervision for language model post-training, but its effectiveness is bottlenecked by teacher quality: external teachers suffer from distribution mismatch, while self-distillation with privileged conditioning is limited by in-context learning capacity. We propose \textbf{RISE} (\textbf{R}ecursive \textbf{I}mprovement via \textbf{S}elf-\textbf{E}xtrapolating Policy Distillation), which constructs a synthetic teacher directly from the model's own RLVR training trajectory. By extrapolating the displacement between the current checkpoint and a trailing anchor---in parameter space or output logit space---RISE converts a sparse outcome-induced parameter update into a dense token-level target, without any external model or privileged conditioning. RISE combines RLVR and OPD in a complementary loop: outcome rewards ground the extrapolation toward correct reasoning, while the extrapolated teacher refines token-level decisions. Moreover, since the teacher is refreshed every iteration as the student improves, distillation becomes a recursive improvement mechanism rather than a one-shot compression step. Experiments spanning mathematical reasoning, multi-domain STEM, code generation, and multi-turn agentic tasks show that RISE outperforms RLVR-only training and on-policy self-distillation across all settings.

</details>


### [325] [LLM-Driven Algorithm Design for Quantum Circuit Synthesis based on Binary Decision Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05327)
*Yoonju Sim,Federico Berto,Chuanbo Hua,Jinkyoo Park,Changhyun Kwon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QuantumEvo是一个利用LLM作为启发式生成器的进化框架，用于发现QCC感知的BDD变量排序启发式。它搜索多个启发式家族初始化的候选方案，而非直接预测排序，最终发现的HGA-QE启发式在基准测试中取得了70.9%的平局或胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可逆电路综合是量子计算中实现布尔函数的关键步骤，BDD提供了可扩展的途径，但电路质量高度依赖变量排序。现有启发式主要最小化BDD大小作为电路规模的代理指标，但BDD大小并不能完美反映合成电路的实际量子成本(QCC)。因此需要更直接的QCC感知优化方法。

Method: 提出QuantumEvo进化框架，使用LLM作为启发式生成器，从多个启发式家族初始化候选方案，在搜索空间中进化发现QCC感知的变量排序策略。候选启发式直接操控变量排序并使用标准BDD操作，通过下游QCC进行选择，而非直接预测排序。最终发现的HGA-QE启发式修改了遗传算法中的sifting步骤，使其与QCC更好对齐。

Result: 在基准测试中，HGA-QE对逐函数最佳基线达到70.9%的平局或胜率，且在13.5%的函数上严格最优。其性能在来自不同源数据的两个基准套件上表现出更明显的严格胜出优势。

Conclusion: 研究表明，使用LLM驱动的进化搜索来发现QCC感知的BDD变量排序启发式是有效的，且该方法的泛化能力在未见过的数据源上表现良好，验证了其鲁棒性和实用性。

Abstract: Quantum circuits are central to implementing quantum algorithms on quantum devices, where quantum gates must be reversible. Many quantum algorithms rely on Boolean functions, which must therefore be implemented reversibly within quantum circuits. Reversible circuit synthesis provides a way to translate such Boolean functions into reversible circuits. Binary decision diagrams (BDDs) offer a scalable approach to this task, but the resulting BDDs and circuits depend heavily on variable ordering. Existing ordering heuristics commonly minimize BDD size because it is closely tied to the circuit size. However, BDD size is an imperfect proxy for the quantum cost of the synthesized circuit (QCC). We propose \texttt{QuantumEvo}, an evolutionary framework that uses an LLM as a heuristic generator for QCC-aware BDD variable ordering. Instead of predicting orderings directly, \texttt{QuantumEvo} searches over ordering heuristics initialized from multiple heuristic families. Candidate heuristics directly manipulate variable orderings using standard BDD operations and are selected by downstream QCC. The discovered heuristic, HGA-QE, modifies the sifting step inside a genetic algorithm so that the procedure is better aligned with QCC. Across the benchmark set, HGA-QE achieves a 70.9\% tie-or-win rate against the per-function best baseline and is strictly best on 13.5\% of the functions. The results demonstrate broadly competitive QCC performance, with HGA-QE showing a clearer relative advantage in strict wins on the two benchmark suites drawn from sources different from the data used for heuristic discovery.

</details>


### [326] [Who Should Grade My Work? Student Perspectives on Transparent AI-Assisted Writing Assessment in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05346)
*Rayed AlGhamdi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过质性教学调查，探讨了沙特某公立大学计算机专业本科生在明确知晓评分与反馈由ChatGPT生成时，如何理解与解读AI介导的评估。研究发现学生认可GenAI反馈对表层修改有用，但在评分决策上仍将人类教师视为权威，体现出"反馈效用"与"评价权威"的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究探讨GenAI生成反馈是否能提升写作表现及学生对其有用性的感知，但鲜有研究关注当学生被明确告知评分与反馈出自AI系统而非人类教师时，他们会如何解读此类评估。本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在一门沙特公立大学面向计算机专业本科生开设的技术写作课程中开展质性教学调查。13名男性本科生完成课堂手写写作任务后，扫描提交的内容由ChatGPT依据与任务目标对齐的rubric提示进行评估。随后明确告知学生评分与反馈由ChatGPT生成，并邀请他们以书面形式反思该评估。研究者对这些反思进行归纳式主题分析。

Result: 归纳式主题分析识别出四个主题：反馈的有用性感知；对AI语境与教学局限性的认知；有条件信任（区分反馈效用与评价权威）；以及对人类教师在制度与教学层面角色的反思。参与者接受GenAI反馈对表层修改有用，但始终将人类教师视为评分决策的合理权威。

Conclusion: 学生将"反馈效用"与"评价权威"视为两个分析上分离的判断，而非单一满意度量表的两端。这一区分对AI辅助评估在高等教育中的设计与实施具有重要意义。

Abstract: The integration of GenAI tools into higher education assessment raises important questions about how students understand, interpret, and respond to AI-mediated evaluation. As instructors increasingly explore AI tools for providing feedback, prior research has examined whether GenAI-generated feedback improves writing performance and how students perceive its usefulness; comparatively little is known, however, about how students interpret such evaluation when they are explicitly informed that an AI system, rather than a human instructor, produced the feedback and the score. This study reports findings from a qualitative pedagogical inquiry conducted in an undergraduate technical communication course for computing students at a Saudi public university. Thirteen male undergraduate computing students completed an in-class handwritten writing task; the scanned submissions were evaluated by ChatGPT using a rubric-based prompt aligned with the task objectives. Students were then explicitly informed that ChatGPT had generated the score and feedback and were invited to reflect on the evaluation in writing. Inductive thematic analysis of these reflections identified four themes: perceived usefulness of feedback; awareness of AI's contextual and pedagogical limitations; conditional trust, distinguishing feedback utility from evaluative authority; and reflection on the institutional and pedagogical role of the human instructor. Participants accepted GenAI feedback as useful for surface-level revision but consistently positioned the human instructor as the appropriate authority over grading decisions. The study identifies this as a distinction between feedback utility and evaluative authority, two judgments that students treat as analytically separate rather than as opposite ends of a single approval scale...

</details>


### [327] [CUA-Universe: A Scalable and Dynamic Environment for Hybrid GUI+CLI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05374)
*Haoting Shi,Wenhao Wang,Weicheng Fang,Yaozhong Liang,Tian Jin,Pengxiang Zhao,Guangyi Liu,Siheng Chen,Yanfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CUA-Universe，一个将真实桌面软件转化为混合GUI+CLI环境的可扩展数据管道，通过App-Forge适配应用、Task-Weave合成混合任务、Path-Steer引导高效轨迹，训练出的9B模型在多个基准上同时显著提升了成功率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的计算机操作是混合的（既需要视觉界面状态检查，也需要精确高效的命令行操作），但现有计算机使用代理仅通过GUI操作，轨迹低效；同时支持GUI和CLI的混合环境由于需要大量手工工程而稀缺。此外，CLI原生代理缺乏视觉感知，GUI原生代理在适合命令操作的任务上效率低下。

Method: 提出CUA-Universe三阶段管道：(1) App-Forge：将应用适配为可复现VM，并通过发现、包装或生成方式创建命令行接口，扩展到16个应用；(2) Task-Weave：从种子文件上的可复用操作合成难度可控的多样化混合任务；(3) Path-Steer：引导rollout沿高效混合路径执行并收集经验轨迹用于后续训练。

Result: 在CUA-Verse上Score +39.3 pts、步骤减少37%、token减少60%；在OSWorld上SR +16.8 pts、步骤减少57%、token减少44%；在OSWorld-MCP上Score +7.84 pts、步骤减少27%、token减少30%。

Conclusion: 训练使代理行为从低效的GUI交互和脆弱的CLI脚本编写转向有效的GUI+CLI协同编排，CUA-Universe为构建更强大且高效的计算机使用代理提供了可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Computer-use agents have advanced on benchmarks like OSWorld and AndroidWorld, but still act mostly through the GUI, often producing inefficient trajectories. Real-world computer work is hybrid, combining visual-state inspection with precise, high-throughput command-line operations, so capable agents must coordinate both modalities over shared application state. Yet scalable hybrid environments remain scarce because supporting both GUI and CLI over real applications typically requires substantial manual engineering for each application. Existing agents also struggle to use the two interfaces complementarily: CLI-native agents lack visual perception for tasks involving interface state or layout, while GUI-native agents are inefficient for operations better executed through commands. We introduce CUA-Universe, a scalable environment-to-data pipeline that turns real desktop software into hybrid GUI+CLI environments. App-Forge adapts applications into reproducible VMs and command-line surfaces it discovers, wraps, or generates, scaling to 16 applications; Task-Weave synthesizes diverse hybrid tasks of controllable difficulty from reusable operations over seed files; and Path-Steer steers rollouts along efficient hybrid paths and harvests verified trajectories for post-training. Training on this data shifts behavior from inefficient GUI interaction and brittle CLI scripting toward effective GUI+CLI orchestration. Our 9B model improves both success and efficiency on CUA-Verse (Score +39.3 pts; -37% steps, -60% tokens), OSWorld (SR +16.8 pts; -57% steps, -44% tokens), and OSWorld-MCP (Score +7.84 pts; -27% steps, -30% tokens). CUA-Universe provides a scalable path toward more capable and efficient computer-use agents.

</details>


### [328] [Molecular Déjà Vu: Digit-Level Retrieval of Published Values in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05381)
*Matthias Busch,Marius Tacke,Sviatlana V. Lamaka,Mikhail L. Zheludkevich,Christian J. Cyron,Roland C. Aydin,Christian Feiler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了22个前沿LLM在12个分子性质回归基准上的逐字检索（verbatim retrieval）现象，发现该现象广泛但具有基准特异性，推理水平会显著影响检索率（高推理水平比最低水平多89%的标记），并测试了一种中断检索的方法来评估模型真实的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在分子性质基准上的评估日益增多，但高准确率无法区分模型是真正预测了分子性质还是仅仅检索了已发表的数值（记忆性检索）。这可能导致对LLM实际预测能力的错误高估，需要系统审计这类逐字检索现象。

Method: 对22个前沿模型在12个回归基准上进行逐字检索审计；在两个推理水平下运行实验以观察推理程度对检索的影响；针对污染最严重的案例设计方法中断检索，测试模型在SMILES字符串转换后是否仍能识别原始标签，并比较抑制检索前后模型预测误差的变化。

Result: 逐字检索广泛存在但具有基准特异性：5个数据集中超过50%的LLM出现逐字检索，其余数据集仅零星出现。推理水平显著影响检索：高推理水平下标记次数比最低水平多89%。中断检索后，最强模型在某些情况下仍能识别变换后的SMILES与原始标签的组合。抑制检索使不同模型的预测误差在相对水平上更接近，而逐字检索的使用差异使各模型彼此拉大。

Conclusion: LLM的泛化预测能力并非仅由记忆的数值量决定。该工作为LLM在分子回归基准中的逐字检索现象提供了系统性的广度与深度概览，提示评估需区分记忆检索与真实预测能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly evaluated on molecular property benchmarks, but accuracy cannot distinguish a model that predicts a property from one that retrieves a published number. We audit 22 frontier models on 12 regression benchmarks for verbatim retrieval and find that it is widespread but relatively benchmark-specific: on five datasets more than $50\%$ of the LLMs show verbatim retrieval, while on the remaining datasets it appears only in isolated cells. We run our experiments at two reasoning levels and find that reasoning changes retrieval. The same experiments, on the same molecules and with the same prompt, are flagged $89\%$ more often at the higher reasoning level than at the lowest one. Finally, we test a way to interrupt retrieval in our most contaminated cases, and find that the strongest models in some cases still recognise a combination of transformed SMILES strings and original labels. Furthermore, suppressing retrieval moves the prediction errors of the different models closer together in relative terms, while their differing use of verbatim retrieval spreads them apart. This indicates that the general predictive capability of an LLM is not determined solely by the amount of memorised values. This work provides an overview of the amount and depth of verbatim retrieval in molecular regression benchmarks using LLMs.

</details>


### [329] [Necessary or Sufficient? Evaluating LLM Explanations With Behavioural Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05385)
*Urja Pawar,Rajitha Ramanayake,Nabeel Kemal,Ashwin Kandath,Owen O'Neill,Guillaume Bourgeon,Houssem Chatbri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM决策组件在解释性输出中提到的“命名因素”是否与其实际决策行为一致。通过两种解释（必要性：改变因素会改变输出；充分性：保留该因素同时移除其他可改信息仍能保持输出），在两类合成任务（顾问推荐和提示词危害性判断）中评估了8个模型（Claude、GPT、Gemini系列）的黑盒干预结果。发现引用因素排序与测量得分的Spearman相关性仅为0.349~0.580，且57.6%/58.1%（顾问）及25.8%/8.9%（监控）的情况下，未被引用的因素分数高于得分最低的被引用因素。说明解释中引用的因素包含有用信息，但无法可靠识别最具实际影响的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的自然语言解释中的命名因素常被操作者用于系统监控、错误诊断或升级决策，这隐含假设解释与模型的真实决策行为一致。但该假设很少被直接检验。作者旨在提供一种黑盒框架来验证解释中命名因素与实际决策行为之间的对齐程度，支持智能体监督场景下的可靠性评估。

Method: 将命名因素的操作性含义量化为两种解释：(1) 必要性——改变该因素是否改变输出；(2) 充分性——保留该因素同时移除其他可改变的信息是否保持输出。通过受控黑盒干预估计每个因素的必要性得分和充分性得分，与模型引用的因素排名计算Spearman相关性。实验涵盖两个合成用例（顾问推荐、提示词危害性/风险判断），8个模型（Claude、GPT、Gemini系列），并额外统计未引用因素得分超过最低引用因素的比例。

Result: 在顾问推荐任务中，引用排名与必要性/充分性得分的平均Spearman相关性分别为0.349和0.354；在提示词监控任务中分别为0.431和0.580。未引用因素得分高于最低引用因素的比例在顾问任务中为57.6%（必要性）和58.1%（充分性），在提示词监控中为25.8%和8.9%。模型引用的前三个因素包含有用信息，但无法可靠识别在必要性或充分性含义下实际影响最强的三个因素。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种面向智能体监督场景的、黑盒的、针对单次LLM决策范围的解释可靠性检查。结果表明当前模型的解释性引用与实际测量影响之间存在系统性差距，解释元素不应对应地被视为可靠的操作依据，需要更严格的校准机制才能支撑安全关键场景的使用。

Abstract: LLM decision components that can operate within agent workflows often produce action-relevant recommendations or judgements together with explanations. Operators may use the named factors to monitor a system, diagnose errors, or decide when to escalate an output. Such use assumes that the explanations agree with the component's observable decision behaviour. We test two interpretations of the named factors: necessity, meaning that changing a factor would change the output, and sufficiency, meaning that retaining it while removing other changeable information would preserve the output. We evaluate these interpretations in two synthetic use cases: recommending advisors to clients and judging prompts for harmfulness or risk. Models return an output and the top three factors that most influenced it. Controlled black-box interventions estimate a necessity score for each factor by measuring how often changing it changes the output, and a sufficiency score by measuring how often retaining it preserves the output. Across eight models from the Claude, GPT, and Gemini families, the mean Spearman correlations between the cited ranking and the necessity and sufficiency scores are 0.349 and 0.354 for advisor recommendation, and 0.431 and 0.580 for prompt monitoring. Furthermore, an uncited factor scores above the lowest-scoring cited factor in 57.6% of advisor responses under necessity and 58.1% under sufficiency; the corresponding prompt-monitoring rates are 25.8% and 8.9%. The cited top three contain useful information but do not reliably identify the three factors with the strongest measured influence under necessity or sufficiency. The framework provides a black-box reliability check for explanations used in agent oversight while remaining scoped to individual LLM decisions.

</details>


### [330] [A Deep Generative Model for Synthesizing Labeled Wireless Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05396)
*Yuxiao Li,Keke Hu,Santiago Mazuelas,Yuan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的IIns-GAN方法（Inter-Instance GAN），用于生成带位置标签的真实感无线信号，解决了无线感知领域真实数据采集成本高、传统合成方法参数调优繁琐且真实性不足的问题。在公共UWB数据集上的实验表明，生成的信号能够真实反映物理特征，并有效提升距离估计和环境识别等无线感知任务的模型训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
